Then we put this equation into
eq.

4.20
to get
eq.

4.27
.

(2) The slope identity
eq.

4.28
comes from
eq.

4.26
, (
4.6
), (
4.7
), and (
4.8
).

(3) We use the expression in
eq.

4.26
with
w:=u^{\prime}-v(\cdot,U,p)
, then we use the quadratic response for
\nu^{*}(U,q)

eq.

4.10
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|U|}\int_{U}\left(\frac{1}{2}\nabla(w-v(\cdot,U,p,q))\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla(w-v(\cdot,U,p,q))\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|U|}\int_{U}\left(\frac{1}{2}\nabla(u^{\prime}-u(\cdot,U,q))\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla(u^{\prime}-u(\cdot,U,q))\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=\nu^{*}(U,q)-\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|U|}\int_{U}\left(-\frac{1}{2}\nabla u^{\prime}\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla u^{\prime}+q\cdot\nabla u^{\prime}\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right].

Then we add back the term
\nu(U,p)
and it gives the desired result

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|U|}\int_{U}\left(\frac{1}{2}\nabla(w-v(\cdot,U,p,q))\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla(w-v(\cdot,U,p,q))\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=J(U,p,q)-\left(\nu(U,p)+\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|U|}\int_{U}\left(-\frac{1}{2}\nabla u^{\prime}\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla u^{\prime}+q\cdot\nabla u^{\prime}\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]-p\cdot q\right)

\displaystyle\qquad=J(U,p,q)-\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|U|}\int_{U}\left(-\frac{1}{2}\nabla w\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla w-p\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla w+q\cdot\nabla w\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right].

(4)
Equation

4.30
is a consequence of
eq.

4.11
and
eq.

4.19
.
∎

We conclude this section with the following lemma.

Lemma 4.4

(Comparison between two scales)
.

For every
n,k\in\mathbb{N}
with
k\leqslant n
, and
p,q\in{\mathbb{R}^{d}}
, writing
v(U)
as shorthand for
v(\cdot,U,p,q)
, we have

(4.31)

\frac{1}{|\mathcal{Z}_{n,k}|}\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,k}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}|}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}}\frac{1}{2}|\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\\
\leqslant J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k},p,q)-J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},p,q).

Proof.

For any
z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,k}
, since
v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})\in\mathcal{A}(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k})
, we use the quadratic response
eq.

4.29
for
J(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k},p,q)
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}|}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}}\frac{1}{2}|\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\leqslant\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}|}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}}\frac{1}{2}(\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}))\cdot\mathbf{a}(\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}))\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\qquad=J(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k},p,q)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}|}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}}\left(-\frac{1}{2}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-p\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})+q\cdot\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right].

We sum this expression over all
z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,k}
to obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\mathcal{Z}_{n,k}|}\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,k}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}|}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}}\frac{1}{2}|\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant\frac{1}{|\mathcal{Z}_{n,k}|}\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,k}}\bigg(J(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k},p,q)

\displaystyle\quad\qquad-\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}|}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k}}\left(-\frac{1}{2}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-p\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})+q\cdot\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad=J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k},p,q)-J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},p,q).

In the last step, we use the stationarity of
J
and also
eq.

4.20
for
v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})
.
∎

5.
Quantitative rate of convergence

We are now ready to prove
Theorem

2.1
. We decompose the argument into a series of four steps.

5.1.
Step 1: setup

We use the shorthand
{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}:={\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})
, so that by
eq.

4.28
, the average slope of the function
v(\cdot,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},p,q)
is
{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}q-p
, in the sense that

(5.1)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\int_{U}\nabla v(\cdot,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},p,q)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle={\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}q-p.

We let
\tau_{n}
denote a measure of the defect in the subadditivity of
J
, precisely,

(5.2)

\begin{split}\tau_{n}:=&\sup_{p,q\in B_{1}}(J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},p,q)-J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n+1},p,q))\\
=&\sup_{p\in B_{1}}(\nu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},p)-\nu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n+1},p))+\sup_{q\in B_{1}}(\nu^{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},q)-\nu^{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n+1},q)).\end{split}

A direct corollary from
eq.

5.2
is that for any integers
n<m
,

(5.3)

\displaystyle|{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}-{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}^{-1}|=\sup_{q\in B_{1}}q\cdot({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}-{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}^{-1})q=\sup_{q\in B_{1}}\left(\nu^{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},q)-\nu^{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},q)\right)\leqslant C\sum_{k=n}^{m-1}\tau_{k}.

We recall that
\{{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})\}_{m\geqslant 0}
is decreasing and
\{{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})\}_{m\geqslant 0}
is increasing, with the comparison
{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})\leqslant{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})
. From
eq.

4.21
, we know that

\displaystyle|{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})-{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}|\leqslant|{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})-{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})|\leqslant C\sup_{p\in B_{1}}(J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p))^{\frac{1}{2}}.

From now on, we thus fix
p\in B_{1}
, and focus on estimating
J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)
. We also assume without further notification that
m
is sufficiently large that
3^{m}\geqslant R_{0}
, for the constant
R_{0}
appearing in
Proposition

3.9
. We use
{{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v(\cdot,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)}
to compare with
eq.

4.27
and apply the quadratic response
eq.

4.29
. In the rest of
Step 1
, we write
v(U)
as a shorthand for
v(\cdot,U,p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)
, and decompose

(5.4)

\begin{split}(J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p))^{\frac{1}{2}}&=\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\frac{1}{2}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\leqslant\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:JamDecom}-a}+\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:JamDecom}-b},\end{split}

with

\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:JamDecom}-a}\\
=\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\frac{1}{2}(\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})-\nabla{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}))\cdot\mathbf{a}(\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})-\nabla{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}))\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

and

\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:JamDecom}-b}=\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\frac{1}{2}\nabla{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

We treat the two terms separately. For
eq.

5.4
-a, since
{{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\in\mathcal{A}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})}
(see
Proposition

A.2
for details), we use
eq.

4.29
to get

\displaystyle|\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:JamDecom}-a}|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad=J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)-\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\left(-\frac{1}{2}\nabla{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\left(-p\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})+{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p\cdot\nabla{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right].

Using Jensen’s inequality, we have

\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\nabla{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\\
\leqslant\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right],

and the conditional expectation also implies that

\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\left(-p\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})+{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p\cdot\nabla{\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\left(-p\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})+{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p\cdot\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right].

Thus we combine these terms with the quadratic response
eq.

4.29
to obtain

\displaystyle|\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:JamDecom}-a}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leqslant J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)-\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\left(-\frac{1}{2}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\qquad-\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\left(-p\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})+{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p\cdot\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\nabla(v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})-v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}))\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla(v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})-v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}))\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right],

and we use
Lemma

4.4
between
{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}
and
{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}
to get

(5.5)

\displaystyle|\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:JamDecom}-a}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leqslant 3^{d}(J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)-J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p))\leqslant C(d,\Lambda)\tau_{m},

where the quantity
\tau_{m}
is defined in
eq.

5.2
.

For the term
eq.

5.4
-b, we can apply the modified Caccioppoli inequality
eq.

3.37
: there exist two finite positive constants
C(d,\Lambda)
and
\theta(d,\Lambda)\in(0,1)
such that

(5.6)

\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\nabla({\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}))\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla({\mathsf{A}}_{3^{m}+2}v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}))\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\\
\leqslant\frac{C}{3^{2m}\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}[(v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}))^{2}]+\theta\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\cdot\mathbf{a}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right].

Using
eq.

4.27
, we see that the averaged gradient term on the right side of
eq.

5.6
is
J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)
, and
eq.

4.30
asserts that
{J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)\leqslant J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)}
. Therefore, we get the bound for
eq.

5.4
-b

(5.7)

\displaystyle|\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:JamDecom}-b}|^{2}\leqslant\frac{C}{3^{2m}\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}[(v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}))^{2}]+\theta J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p).

We put
eq.

5.5
and
eq.

5.7
back to
eq.

5.4
, obtaining

\displaystyle(J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p))^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\tau_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\frac{C}{3^{2m}\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\left\|{v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})}\right\|^{2}_{\mathscr{L}^{2}}+\theta J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\tau_{m}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\frac{C}{3^{m}(\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left\|{v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})}\right\|_{\mathscr{L}^{2}}+\theta^{\frac{1}{2}}(J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p))^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Since
\theta<1
, this gives

(5.8)

\displaystyle J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)\leqslant C\left(\tau_{m}+\frac{1}{3^{2m}\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\left\|{v(\mu,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)}\right\|^{2}_{\mathscr{L}^{2}}\right).

5.2.
Step 2: flatness estimate

In this step, we estimate the
\mathscr{L}^{2}
-flatness of optimizers of
J
. Notice that, using the result of Lemma
5.10
with
v(\cdot,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)
, the corresponding affine function is
0
and we obtain from
eq.

5.8
that

(5.9)

\displaystyle J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},p,{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}p)\leqslant C\left(3^{-\beta m}+\sum_{n=0}^{m}3^{-\beta(m-n)}\tau_{n}\right).

Lemma 5.1

(
\mathscr{L}^{2}
-flatness estimate)
.

There exist
\beta(d)>0
and
C(d,\Lambda,\rho)<\infty
such that for every
p,q\in B_{1}
and
m\in\mathbb{N}
,

(5.10)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\left\|{v(\cdot,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1},p,q)-\ell_{\overline{\mathbf{a}}_{m}^{-1}q-p,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}}}\right\|^{2}_{\mathscr{L}^{2}}\leqslant C3^{2m}\left(3^{-\beta m}+\sum_{n=0}^{m}3^{-\beta(m-n)}\tau_{n}\right).

Proof.

In the rest of the proof, we write
v(U):=v(\cdot,U,p,q)
as we will not change
p,q
in the proof. Since
\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})-\ell_{\overline{\mathbf{a}}_{m}^{-1}q-p,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}}|\mathcal{G}_{m+1}\right]=0
, we can use the multiscale Poincaré inequality
eq.

3.23

(5.11)

\begin{split}&\frac{1}{(\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left\|{v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})-\ell_{\overline{\mathbf{a}}_{m}^{-1}q-p,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}}}\right\|_{\mathscr{L}^{2}}\\
&\leqslant C\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}}|\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\qquad+C\sum_{n=0}^{m+1}3^{n}\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}}|\mathsf{S}_{m+1,n}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

The first term on the right side above is of constant order, by
eq.

4.27
. For the second term, we use a two-scale comparison for every
0\leqslant n\leqslant m+1
that

(5.12)

\begin{split}&\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}}|\mathsf{S}_{m+1,n}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\leqslant|{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}^{-1}-{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}|+\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m+1,n}}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}|\mathsf{S}_{m+1,n}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})-\mathsf{S}_{m+1,n}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\qquad+\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m+1,n}}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}|\mathsf{S}_{m+1,n}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

For the first term
|{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}^{-1}-{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}|
we have

\displaystyle|{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}^{-1}-{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}|^{2}\leqslant C(d,\Lambda)|{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}^{-1}-{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}|\leqslant\sum_{k=n}^{m-1}\tau_{k}.

For the second term in
eq.

5.12
, recalling
eq.

3.15
, we use Jensen’s inequality and
eq.

4.31
to get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m+1,n}}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}|\mathsf{S}_{m+1,n}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})-\mathsf{S}_{m+1,n}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m+1,n}}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}|\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})-\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant\sum_{k=n}^{m}\tau_{k}.

For the third term
eq.

5.12
, we use
eq.

3.17
, Jensen’s inequality,
and stationarity. Here we remark that the operator
\mathsf{S}_{n,n}^{z}
is a conditional expectation with more information than
\mathsf{S}_{m+1,n}
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m+1,n}}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}|\mathsf{S}_{m+1,n}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m+1,n}}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}|\mathsf{S}_{n,n}^{z}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}|\mathsf{S}_{n}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right].

The estimation of this term is postponed to the next step. We will prove in
Lemma

5.2
below that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}|\mathsf{S}_{n}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\leqslant C3^{-\beta n}+\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}3^{-\beta(n-k)}\tau_{k}.

We put these estimates back to
eq.

5.11
and obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|)^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left\|{v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})-\ell_{{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}^{-1}q-p,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}}}\right\|_{\mathscr{L}^{2}}\leqslant C\sum_{n=0}^{m}3^{n}\left(3^{-\beta n}+\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}3^{-\beta(n-k)}\tau_{k}+\sum_{k=n}^{m}\tau_{k}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

We square the two sides and use the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}|}\left\|{v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1})-\ell_{{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{m}^{-1}q-p,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m+1}}}\right\|_{\mathscr{L}^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\leqslant C\left(\sum_{n=0}^{m}3^{n}\right)\left(\sum_{n=0}^{m}3^{n}\left(3^{-\beta n}+\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}3^{-\beta(n-k)}\tau_{k}+\sum_{k=n}^{m}\tau_{k}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\leqslant C3^{2m}\left(3^{-\beta m}+\sum_{n=0}^{m}3^{-\beta(m-n)}\tau_{n}\right),

as announced.
∎

5.3.
Step 3: variance estimate

In this part, we prove the following variance estimate, which was used in Step 2.

Lemma 5.2

(Variance estimate)
.

There exist
\beta(d)>0
and
C(d,\Lambda,\rho)<\infty
such that for every
p,q\in B_{1}
and
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

(5.13)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}|\mathsf{S}_{n}\nabla v(\mu,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},p,q)-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\leqslant C3^{-\beta n}+\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}3^{-\beta(n-k)}\tau_{k}.

Proof.

In the rest of the proof, we write
v(U):=v(\cdot,U,p,q)
, as we will not change
p,q
in the proof. From
eq.

5.1
, we know that the average slope of
v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})
is
({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}q-p)
, and notice that
v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})
is
\mathcal{F}_{B_{1}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}
-measurable. Thus the idea is to use
\{v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k})\}_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,k}}
to approximate
eq.

5.13
in scale
3^{k}
with some error, and then apply the independence for
v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k})
and
v(z^{\prime}+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{k})
for
\dist(z,z^{\prime})
large. However, different from the standard elliptic setting, here we will see a renormalization with random weights.

We start by relaxing
eq.

5.13
to
\mathcal{G}_{n,n-2}
. We observe that in fact
\mathsf{S}_{n}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})
is constant in
{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}
, so

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}|\mathsf{S}_{n}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu=\frac{1}{\mu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}\left|\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}\left(\mathsf{S}_{n}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}q-p)\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right|^{2}.

We denote by
V_{n}
the left hand side of
eq.

5.13
. By triangle inequality, we have

(5.14)

\displaystyle(V_{n})^{\frac{1}{2}}\leqslant\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:mainVarianceDecom}-a}+\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:mainVarianceDecom}-b}+\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:mainVarianceDecom}-c},

with

eq.

5.14
-a

\displaystyle=|{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n}^{-1}-{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n-2}^{-1}|,

eq.

5.14
-b

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\frac{1}{\mu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}\left|\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}}\left(\mathsf{S}_{n,n}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-\mathsf{S}_{n,n-2}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right|^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

eq.

5.14
-c

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\frac{1}{\mu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}\left|\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}}\left(\mathsf{S}_{n,n-2}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n-2}^{-1}q-p)\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right|^{2}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

The term
eq.

5.14
-a can be controlled by
eq.

5.3
:

(5.15)

\displaystyle\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:mainVarianceDecom}-a}\leqslant C(\tau_{n-2}+\tau_{n-1})^{\frac{1}{2}}.

For the term
eq.

5.14
-b, recalling
eq.

3.17
and
eq.

3.15
, we use Jensen’s inequality
and the two-scale comparison
eq.

4.31
to get

(5.16)

\begin{split}\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:mainVarianceDecom}-b}&\leqslant\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}}|\mathsf{S}_{n,n}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-\mathsf{S}_{n,n-2}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\leqslant\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}}|\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})-\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\leqslant(\tau_{n-2}+\tau_{n-1})^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

The term
eq.

5.14
-c is the key for our result. To simplify a little more the notation, we write

(5.17)

\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}X_{z}:=\mathsf{S}_{n,n-2}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})(\mu,z)-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n-2}^{-1}q-p),\\
m_{z}:=\mu(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}).\end{array}\right.

Notice that
X_{z},m_{z}
are
\mathcal{F}_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-1}}
-measurable. With this notation in place, we have

\displaystyle\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}}\left(\mathsf{S}_{n,n-2}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n-2}^{-1}q-p)\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu

\displaystyle=m_{z}X_{z},

and by
eq.

5.1
,

\mathbb{E}_{\rho}[m_{z}X_{z}]=0.

The term
eq.

5.14
-c we want to estimate can be rewritten as

eq.

5.14
-c

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\frac{\left(\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}X_{z}\right)^{2}}{\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

If the coefficients
m_{z}
were deterministic, then we would be able to leverage on the finite range of dependence of
X_{z}
in this variance term.
However, since the number of particles
m_{z}
is random, we introduce the event

(5.18)

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}_{n,\rho,\delta}:=\left\{\mu\in\mathcal{M}_{\delta}({\mathbb{R}^{d}}):\forall z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2},\left|\frac{\mu(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}|}-1\right|\leqslant\delta,\text{ and }\left|\frac{\mu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}-1\right|\leqslant\delta\right\},

thus we can divide
eq.

5.14
-c into two terms

eq.

5.14
-c

\displaystyle\leqslant\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:mainVarianceDecom}-c1}+\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:mainVarianceDecom}-c2},

eq.

5.14
-c1

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}_{\left\{(\mathcal{C}_{n,\rho,\delta})^{c}\right\}}}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\frac{\left(\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}X_{z}\right)^{2}}{\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

eq.

5.14
-c2

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}_{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{n,\rho,\delta}\right\}}}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\frac{\left(\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}X_{z}\right)^{2}}{\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

For the term
eq.

5.14
-c1, we know that
(\mathcal{C}_{n,\rho,\delta})^{c}
is not typical in large scales, and we have the Chernoff bound

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\rho}[\mu\notin\mathcal{C}_{n,\rho,\delta}]\leqslant 3^{2d+1}\exp\left(-\frac{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}|\delta^{2}}{4}\right).

Moreover, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\frac{\left(\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}X_{z}\right)^{2}}{\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}}\leqslant\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}|X_{z}|^{2}.

We need a bound for the term
|X_{z}|^{2}
: recalling the definition in
eq.

3.15
and
eq.

4.28
,

\displaystyle\mathsf{S}_{n-2,n-2}^{z}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})(\mu,z)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\strokedint_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\ \Bigg|\ \mathcal{G}_{n-2,n-2}^{z}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{a}(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2};\mathcal{G}_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}})^{-1}q-p.

Using the martingale structure of
eq.

3.17
, we have

\displaystyle X_{z}

\displaystyle=\mathsf{S}_{n,n-2}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})(\mu,z)-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n-2}^{-1}q-p)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\strokedint_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}}\mathsf{S}_{n-2,n-2}^{z}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\ \Bigg|\ \mathcal{G}_{n,n-2}\right]-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n-2}^{-1}q-p)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\mathbf{a}(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2};\mathcal{G}_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}})^{-1}-{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n-2}^{-1}\ \Bigg|\ \mathcal{G}_{n,n-2}\right]q.

Then we use Jensen’s inequality and the bound of
{\mathsf{Id}\leqslant\mathbf{a}(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2};\mathcal{G}_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}})\leqslant\Lambda\mathsf{Id}}

(5.19)

\begin{split}|X_{z}|^{2}&=|\mathsf{S}_{n,n-2}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n-2}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\\
&=\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[|\mathbf{a}(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2};\mathcal{G}_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}})-{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n-2}^{-1}|^{2}\,|\,\mathcal{G}_{n,n-2}\right]\leqslant\Lambda^{2}.\end{split}

This concludes that

(5.20)

\begin{split}\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:mainVarianceDecom}-c1}\leqslant\Lambda^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}_{\left\{(\mathcal{C}_{n,\rho,\delta})^{c}\right\}}}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\mu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})\right]&\leqslant C(d,\Lambda)\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}|}\exp\left(-\frac{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}|\delta^{2}}{4}\right)\\
&\leqslant C(d,\Lambda,\rho)3^{-dn}.\end{split}

Finally, we treat
eq.

5.14
-c2. We calculate
eq.

5.14
-c2 at first with the conditional expectation with respect to
\mathcal{G}_{n,n-2}
. Clearly,
\mathcal{C}_{n,\rho,\delta}
is
\mathcal{G}_{n,n-2}
-measurable, and under this condition
{\mu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})\geqslant(1-\delta)\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}
, so we have

(5.21)

\begin{split}|\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:mainVarianceDecom}-c2}|^{2}&=\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{\mathbf{1}_{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{n,\rho,\delta}\right\}}}{\mu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\left(\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}X_{z}\right)^{2}\ \Bigg|\ \mathcal{G}_{n,n-2}\right]\right]\\
&\leqslant\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{(1-\delta)\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\mathbf{1}_{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{n,\rho,\delta}\right\}}\left(\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}X_{z}\right)^{2}\ \Bigg|\ \mathcal{G}_{n,n-2}\right]\right].\end{split}

We would like to develop the term
|\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}X_{z}|^{2}
and also drop out the indicator term. The argument here is deterministic

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}X_{z}\right|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z,z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}\\
|z-z^{\prime}|_{\infty}<3^{n-1}\end{subarray}}m_{z}m_{z^{\prime}}X_{z}\cdot X_{z^{\prime}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z,z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}\\
|z-z^{\prime}|_{\infty}\geqslant 3^{n-1}\end{subarray}}m_{z}m_{z^{\prime}}X_{z}\cdot X_{z^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z,z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}\\
|z-z^{\prime}|_{\infty}<3^{n-1}\end{subarray}}\left((m_{z})^{2}|X_{z}|^{2}+(m_{z^{\prime}})^{2}|X_{z^{\prime}}|^{2}\right)+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z,z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}\\
|z-z^{\prime}|_{\infty}\geqslant 3^{n-1}\end{subarray}}m_{z}m_{z^{\prime}}X_{z}\cdot X_{z^{\prime}},

where
|z-z^{\prime}|_{\infty}:=\max_{1\leqslant i\leqslant d}|z_{i}-z^{\prime}_{i}|
. We now add back the indicator
\mathbf{1}_{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{n,\rho,\delta}\right\}}
and develop it

(5.22)

\begin{split}&\mathbf{1}_{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{n,\rho,\delta}\right\}}\left|\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}X_{z}\right|^{2}\\
&\leqslant\mathbf{1}_{\left\{\mathcal{C}_{n,\rho,\delta}\right\}}\left(\frac{(1+\delta)\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}|}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z,z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}\\
|z-z^{\prime}|<3^{n-1}\end{subarray}}\left(m_{z}|X_{z}|^{2}+m_{z^{\prime}}|X_{z^{\prime}}|^{2}\right)+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z,z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}\\
|z-z^{\prime}|\geqslant 3^{n-1}\end{subarray}}m_{z}m_{z^{\prime}}X_{z}\cdot X_{z^{\prime}}\right)\\
&\leqslant\frac{(1+\delta)\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}|}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z,z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}\\
|z-z^{\prime}|<3^{n-1}\end{subarray}}\left(m_{z}|X_{z}|^{2}+m_{z^{\prime}}|X_{z^{\prime}}|^{2}\right)+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z,z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}\\
|z-z^{\prime}|\geqslant 3^{n-1}\end{subarray}}m_{z}m_{z^{\prime}}X_{z}\cdot X_{z^{\prime}}.\end{split}

From the first line to the second line above, we use that
m_{z}\leqslant(1+\delta)\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}|
under the event
\mathcal{C}_{n,\rho,\delta}
. We then keep in mind that the quantity in
\Big(\cdots\Big)
on the second line of
eq.

5.22
is always larger than
\left|\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}}m_{z}X_{z}\right|^{2}
, so it is nonnegative. Therefore, from the second line to the third line, we can drop the indicator function in front. Inserting this estimate into
eq.

5.21
, we obtain that

\displaystyle|\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:mainVarianceDecom}-c2}|^{2}\leqslant\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\frac{(1+\delta)|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}|}{(1-\delta)|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z,z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}\\
|z-z^{\prime}|_{\infty}<3^{n-1}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{2}\left(m_{z}|X_{z}|^{2}+m_{z^{\prime}}|X_{z^{\prime}}|^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\frac{1}{(1-\delta)|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z,z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}\\
|z-z^{\prime}|_{\infty}\geqslant 3^{n-1}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[m_{z}m_{z^{\prime}}X_{z}\cdot X_{z^{\prime}}\right].

The sum in the second line is
0
, because for
|z-z^{\prime}|_{\infty}\geqslant 3^{n-1}
,
m_{z}X_{z}
and
m_{z^{\prime}}X_{z^{\prime}}
are independent,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[m_{z}m_{z^{\prime}}X_{z}\cdot X_{z^{\prime}}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[m_{z}X_{z}\right]\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[m_{z^{\prime}}X_{z^{\prime}}\right]=0.

For the sum in the first line,
\mathbb{E}_{\rho}[m_{z}|X_{z}|^{2}]
is nothing but

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[{\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}}|\mathsf{S}_{n,n-2}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n-2}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,d\mu}\right].

We use Jensen’s inequality
to shrink the operator to
\mathsf{S}_{n-2,n-2}^{z}
that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[{\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}}|\mathsf{S}_{n,n-2}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n-2}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,d\mu}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\leqslant\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[{\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}}|\mathsf{S}_{n-2,n-2}^{z}\nabla v(z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n-2}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,d\mu}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[{\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2}}|\mathsf{S}_{n-2}\nabla v({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n-2})-({\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{n-2}^{-1}q-p)|^{2}\,d\mu}\right].

There are at most
9^{d}\times 5^{d}
pairs
z,z^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z}_{n,n-2}
such that
|z-z^{\prime}|_{\infty}<3^{n-1}
; see
Figure

3
for an illustration. Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle|\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:mainVarianceDecom}-c2}|^{2}
