\displaystyle=\frac{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\sum_{z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n}\cap(Q_{3^{m}}\backslash Q_{3^{m}-2\times 3^{n}})}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}\int_{z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}\left|{p}+\nabla\phi(\mu,y,z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{|Q_{3^{m}}\backslash Q_{3^{m}-2\times 3^{n}}|}{|Q_{3^{m}}|}\nu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})

\displaystyle\leqslant 3^{-(m-n)}\Lambda|{p}|^{2}.

For the second term
eq.

B.20
-b, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{Q_{3^{m}}\backslash Q_{3^{m}-2\times 3^{n}}}\left|\nabla\phi_{{p},m,n}(\mu,y)-\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{Q_{3^{m}}}\left|\nabla\phi_{{p},m,n}(\mu,y)-\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\right]

\displaystyle=\nu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})-\nu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p}).

Therefore, when we take
m,n\to\infty
, both
eq.

B.20
-a and
eq.

B.20
-b go to
0
, so the boundary layer in a mecroscopic scale can be neglected.
∎

Now that the gradient of the whole-space corrector
\nabla\phi_{p}
is well-defined, we can proceed to complete the proof of Theorem
B.2
.

Proof of parts (2) and (3) of Theorem
B.2
.

We start by discussing the validity of the identities
eq.

B.10
and
eq.

B.11
. We use the stationary approximate corrector
\widetilde{\phi}_{{p},n}
defined in
eq.

B.6
, and observe from
eq.

B.17
and Theorem
B.1
that

\displaystyle{p}\cdot{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}{p}

\displaystyle=\lim_{m\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[({p}+\nabla\widetilde{\phi}_{{p},m}(\mu+\delta_{0},0))\cdot\mathbf{a}(\mu+\delta_{0},0)({p}+\nabla\widetilde{\phi}_{{p},m}(\mu+\delta_{0},0))\right]

\displaystyle=\lim_{m\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{0}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{0}}({p}+\nabla\widetilde{\phi}_{{p},m})\cdot\mathbf{a}({p}+\nabla\widetilde{\phi}_{{p},m})\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right].

The identity
eq.

B.10
then follows from the convergence of
{\nabla\widetilde{\phi}_{{p},m}}
to
{\nabla\phi_{{p}}}
in
(\mathscr{L}^{2}_{\bullet,\mathrm{loc}})^{d}
. For the second identity, we can use the fact that

\displaystyle\lim_{m\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\mathbf{a}\left({p}+\nabla\phi(\mu,\cdot,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]={\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}{p},

the estimate
eq.

B.8
, and the stationarity of
\nabla\phi_{p}
. The identity
eq.

B.9
can also be deduced from
eq.

B.8
because

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}|\nabla\phi^{*}(\mu,\cdot,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})-\nabla\phi_{{p}}(\mu,\cdot)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant 2\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}|\nabla\phi(\mu,\cdot,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})-\nabla\phi_{{p}}(\mu,\cdot)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+2\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}|\nabla\phi^{*}(\mu,\cdot,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})-\nabla\phi(\mu,\cdot,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right].

By
eq.

4.26
and
eq.

4.27
, the second term can be bounded by
J({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p},{\overbracket{\mathbf{a}}}_{*}({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}){p})
; and by
eq.

4.19
and
eq.

B.2
, this quantity converges to
0
as
m\to\infty
. From now on, we thus focus on the proof of
eq.

B.8
.

The idea of the proof of
eq.

B.8
is very close to that for
eq.

B.7
and
eq.

B.19
. We fix a mescroscopic scale
n=\lfloor\frac{m}{3}\rfloor
, and then use the decomposition

(B.21)

\begin{split}&\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}|\nabla\phi_{{p}}(\mu,\cdot)-\nabla\phi(\mu,\cdot,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]\\
&\leqslant\underbrace{2\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}|\nabla\phi_{{p}}(\mu,\cdot)-\nabla\widetilde{\phi}_{{p},n}(\mu,\cdot)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]}_{\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:AppCorrectorDecom}-a}}\\
&\qquad+\underbrace{2\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}|\nabla\widetilde{\phi}_{{p},n}(\mu,\cdot)-\nabla\phi(\mu,\cdot,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]}_{\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:AppCorrectorDecom}-b}}.\end{split}

For the first term
eq.

B.21
-a, we use the stationarity to transform to the integration on unit cube
{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{0}
, and then use the fact that
{\nabla\widetilde{\phi}_{{p},n}}
converges to
{\nabla\phi_{{p}}}
in
(\mathscr{L}^{2}_{\bullet,\mathrm{loc}})^{d}
to get that

\displaystyle\lim_{m\to\infty}\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:AppCorrectorDecom}-a}=\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{0}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{0}}|\nabla\widetilde{\phi}_{{p},n}(\mu,\cdot)-\nabla\phi_{{p}}(\mu,\cdot)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu\right]=0.

Thus it suffices to finish the second term
eq.

B.21
-b. We use the definition in
eq.

B.6
and Jensen’s inequality to get that

eq.

B.21
-b

\displaystyle=2\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\left|\left(\strokedint_{y+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}\nabla\phi(\mu,y,x+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})\,{\mathrm{d}}x\right)-\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant 2\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}\strokedint_{y+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}\left|\nabla\phi(\mu,y,x+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})-\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant 2\times 3^{-dn}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{Q_{3^{m}+3^{n}}}\int_{x+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}\left|\nabla\phi(\mu,y,x+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})-\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant 2\times\left(\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:AppCorrectorDecom}-b1}+\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:AppCorrectorDecom}-b2}+\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:AppCorrectorDecom}-b3}\right),

where in the last line we decompose once again the integration into three terms with respect to the domain

eq.

B.21
-b1

\displaystyle=3^{-dn}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{Q_{3^{m}-10\times 3^{n}}}\int_{x+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}\left|\nabla\phi(\mu,y,x+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})-\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\right],

eq.

B.21
-b2

\displaystyle=3^{-dn}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{Q_{3^{m}+3^{n}}\backslash Q_{3^{m}-10\times 3^{n}}}\int_{x+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}\left|{p}+\nabla\phi(\mu,y,x+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\right],

eq.

B.21
-b3

\displaystyle=3^{-dn}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{Q_{3^{m}+3^{n}}\backslash Q_{3^{m}-10\times 3^{n}}}\int_{x+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}\left|{p}+\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\,{\mathrm{d}}x\right].

The terms
eq.

B.21
-b2 and
eq.

B.21
-b3 are easy to treat as they are boundary layer terms. For
eq.

B.21
-b2 we can use the energy bound

\displaystyle\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:AppCorrectorDecom}-b2}\leqslant C3^{-(m-n)}\nu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})\xrightarrow[m\to\infty]{}0.

For
eq.

B.21
-b3, since the function
\left|{p}+\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right|^{2}
does not involve
x
, we use Fubini’s lemma that

eq.

B.21
-b3

\displaystyle=3^{-dn}\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{Q_{3^{m}+3^{n}}}\left(\int_{Q_{3^{m}+3^{n}}\backslash Q_{3^{m}-10\times 3^{n}}}\mathbf{1}_{\left\{x\in y+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}\right\}}\,{\mathrm{d}}x\right)\left|{p}+\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{Q_{3^{m}+3^{n}}\backslash Q_{3^{m}-20\times 3^{n}}}\left|{p}+\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{Q_{3^{m}}\backslash Q_{3^{m}-20\times 3^{n}}}\left|{p}+\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\right]+\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{Q_{3^{m}+3^{n}}\backslash Q_{3^{m}}}\left|p\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\right]

\displaystyle\xrightarrow[m\to\infty]{}0.

Here from the second line to the third line, we use the fact that the gradient contributes only on
Q_{3^{m}+3^{n}}\backslash Q_{3^{m}-20\times 3^{n}}
. Then we do a decomposition: the integration on
Q_{3^{m}+3^{n}}\backslash Q_{3^{m}}
can be calculated directly, since
\phi(\mu,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})
is
\mathcal{F}_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}
-measurable and the gradient vanishes; the integration on
Q_{3^{m}+3^{n}}\backslash Q_{3^{m}-20\times 3^{n}}
can be bounded by the boundary layer estimate in
Lemma

B.6
.

Finally, we focus on
eq.

B.21
-b1. We rewrite the integration
{\int_{Q_{3^{m}-10\times 3^{n}}}}
as

\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:AppCorrectorDecom}-b1}\\
\leqslant\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\strokedint_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n}\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})\geqslant 5\times 3^{n}\end{subarray}}\int_{x+z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}\left|\nabla\phi(\mu,y,x+z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})-\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\,\right){\mathrm{d}}x\right].

For each fixed
x\in{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}
, we can propose a sub-minimizer
\ell_{{p},{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}+w_{x}
for
\nu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})
defined by (see
Figure

6
for an illustration)

\displaystyle w_{x}:=\phi(\cdot,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}\backslash U_{x},{p})+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n}\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})\geqslant 5\times 3^{n}\end{subarray}}\phi(\cdot,x+z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p}),

where

\displaystyle U_{x}:=\overline{\bigcup_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n}\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})\geqslant 5\times 3^{n}\end{subarray}}(x+z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n})}.

The gradient of
w_{x}
and
\phi(\cdot,x+z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})
coincides on every cube
x+z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}
, so we can write

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n}\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})\geqslant 5\times 3^{n}\end{subarray}}\int_{x+z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}}\left|\nabla\phi(\mu,y,x+z+{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})-\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})\right|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\,\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant\mathbb{E}_{\rho}\left[\frac{1}{\rho|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\int_{{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}}|\nabla w_{x}(\mu,y)-\nabla\phi(\mu,y,{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\mu(y)\right]

\displaystyle\leqslant\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}z\in\mathcal{Z}_{m,n}\\
\dist(z,\partial{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m})\geqslant 5\times 3^{n}\end{subarray}}\frac{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n}|}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\nu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})+\frac{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}\backslash U_{x}|}{|{\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}|}\nu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m}\backslash U_{x},{p})\right)-\nu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})

\displaystyle\leqslant\nu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{n},{p})-\nu({\mathchoice{\scalebox{1.2}{$\displaystyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\textstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptstyle\square$}}{\scalebox{1.2}{$\scriptscriptstyle\square$}}}_{m},{p})+5\times 3^{-\frac{2m}{3}}\Lambda|{p}|^{2},

where we used the quadratic response (
4.9
) from the second line to the third line. This implies that
\lim_{m\to\infty}\text{\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:AppCorrectorDecom}-b1}=0
, and thus completes the proof of
eq.

B.8
.
∎

Figure 6.
The function
\ell_{{p},\square_{m}}+w_{x}
is a sub-minimizer for the problem
\nu(\square_{m},{p})
, which combines the minimizer in cubes of scale
3^{n}
biased by a vector
x
(the cubes in blue), and a minimizer in
U_{x}
(the domain in red).
